# Independent-interest review of every result outside the closure of Theorem 1.1

Date: 2026-10-07/08. Method: `notes/PLAN_RESTRUCTURING_3.md` §1.3. One classifier agent per cluster read every
unused item (statement, proof, surrounding text, the paper, HOL and the AFP); one adversarial verifier per
cluster argued the opposite of each verdict and changed it where the evidence required. Verdicts:

- **KEEP_ROOT**: of independent interest (a paper statement, a classical/named theorem, or a general result absent from HOL/AFP); becomes a root.
- **KEEP_EVIDENCE**: faithfulness evidence; displayed in `Paper_Readings`.
- **FIX_THEN_KEEP**: of independent interest but in the wrong form or place; kept now, fixed in a later phase.
- **KEEP_API**: natural interface of a constant a library keeps.
- **COROLLARY**: a special case of a live or kept result; becomes a one-line corollary where the name is useful, otherwise goes.
- **DELETE**: helper of something deleted, abandoned route, twin of live or library code, or superseded paper-specific variant.

Line counts are estimated from the item ranges.

## class_dp

**Classifier summary.** The one item here of real independent interest is the dynamic programming principle, Prop. 2.4 of the paper, which the session promises (root.tex: 'proved here in full'). exit_val_dpp_le_of_cond (120 lines) holds its missing <= half and, in its inner `key` step, the attainment clause. Once its hypothesis is discharged by the live exit_val_cond_time and r is replaced by a stopping time theta, it gives Eq. (2.9) as an equality, attained by every optimizer, together with the live exit_val_dpp_sup_ge_time. Verdict FIX_THEN_KEEP; it should feed a declared Prop. 2.4 root (plan D4), needing the dead pexit_cap_eq.

Two Statement items are faithfulness evidence:
- the class bridge paper_class_marginal and paper_class_lift: KEEP_EVIDENCE, displayed in Paper_Readings from their live originals;
- convex_sets_are_expandable: FIX_THEN_KEEP, restated as the paper's own Section 4 claim (convex ⇒ paper_expandable).

kernel_class_LP_measurable is not a headline, but it removes hypothesis Kb from the live exit_class_kglue_law' and a measurability bridge that is built by hand three times. FIX_THEN_KEEP, as a paper-free CPS lemma plus a 5-line instance.

bm_increment_cross is a closure false positive: its two named conclusions are used by live code. KEEP_API, moving with the Brownian block to Wiener_Measure.

Everything else is dead weight:
- the whole Dynamic_Programming_Kernels island (mixed kernel glue, the simple-stopping-time induction with dpp_chain/dpp_disj, the r.c.d. at a stopping time). Every result there is a special case of the live stopping-time additive-glue route, or an unfinished route.
- the elementary-conditioning chain exit_class_future_of_past, superseded by the r.c.d. form exit_class_rcd_member.
- the market bridge lemma, exit_val_paste_ge, and the dead Statement re-exports.
Several small facts are corollaries of live results: exit_val_horizon_stable from exit_val_horizon_cap, exit_val_horizon_zero from exit_val_le_T, exit_class_sets_prob_algebra from compactness, plus exit_class_Y_entry_measurable, clause_0_not_infinite and clause_2_supersolution. Only exit_val_eq_covariation_val is worth keeping: a 2-line G-B bridge, which also gives monotonicity of v in L (Remark 1.1(d)).

Line totals (1618 in all):
- KEEP_ROOT: 0 (but exit_val_dpp_le_of_cond becomes the core of the new Prop. 2.4 root)
- FIX_THEN_KEEP: 184 (exit_val_dpp_le_of_cond 120, kernel_class_LP_measurable 58, convex_sets_are_expandable 6)

So about 1390 lines go and about 230 stay.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound. 19 of 20 items are confirmed. One verdict changes (kernel_class_LP_measurable), every listed line is covered, and there are no overlaps.

Confirmed after adversarial checks:
- exit_val_dpp_le_of_cond, FIX_THEN_KEEP. It is the missing ≤ half and the attainment clause of the paper's Prop. 2.4 (EM_final.tex:376-386). The proposed fix is correct:
  - exit_val_cond_time (live) needs no stopping-time property.
  - Off survival, Path_Exit_Times.pexit_cap_eq at r := θ ω applies because 0 ≤ θ ≤ T.
  - With the live exit_val_dpp_sup_ge_time this gives equality at measurable path stopping times.
  - Attainment follows from `key` plus exit_val_attained.
- convex_sets_are_expandable, FIX_THEN_KEEP. This is the paper's own claim in the remark after Prop. 4.1 (EM_final.tex:779). The direct proof of paper_expandable with R i = mat 1 and b i = (1-i)·x0 is short via HOL Starlike.mem_interior_convex_shrink. It needs neither compactness nor Test_Functions.convex_expandable, because `expandable` then follows from paper_expandable_imp_expandable. So the classifier's "needs" entry is unnecessary.
- paper_class_marginal / paper_class_lift, KEEP_EVIDENCE. The originals iexit_class_marginal_in_xclass and xclass_lift_in_iexit_class are live (USED); only the re-export names are dead.
- bm_increment_cross, KEEP_API. This is a closure false positive: its named conclusions are USED by live code. bm_paths and BMP already live in Wiener_Measure.Product_Brownian_Motion.
- exit_val_eq_covariation_val, COROLLARY kept conditional on G-B. Confirmed; its needs are Pair_Path_Laws covariation_val_def and covariation_val_mono_class.

Every DELETE survived the attempt to argue independent interest:
- exit_class_kglue_mixed is the Nutz–van Handel / El Karoui–Tan "stability under pasting on an F_r-event". In law it is the live exit_class_aglue / exit_class_aglue_selector at the stopping time θ' = r on A, T off A.
- exit_val_dpp_ge_const_two, dpp_ge_step, dpp_chain and dpp_disj are subsumed by the live stopping-time ≥ half.
- AE_rcd_stopping_diffquot_* belong to an unfinished, sconstraint-specific route.
- exit_class_future_of_past is the event form of the live r.c.d. form exit_class_rcd_member. The latter's proof uses neither it nor its pfut_law_* helpers.
- clause_2_subsolution: the local/ball predicate with F is equivalent to the K-global env predicate at interior points. F is lsc, because the feasible set shrinks for p ≠ 0, so F_* = F. The test_fun_at test functions can be redefined away from x, so local and global touching agree for u bounded above. It is therefore only a predicate variant of the live clause_3 route.
- exit_val_horizon_stable is a corollary of exit_val_horizon_cap and exit_val_le_ball_bound for L ≥ 1. For L < 1, sconstraint k L is empty and the statement is trivial.
- exit_class_sets_prob_algebra, exit_val_horizon_zero, exit_class_Y_entry_measurable, exit_val_paste_ge, stopped_market_acov_leaves_sconstraint, paper_value_function_agrees, clause_0_not_infinite and clause_2_supersolution are as the classifier states.

## comparison

**Classifier summary.** The one item here of independent interest is Theorem 4.2(a), max_principle_boundary_holds, which the paper explicitly calls a theorem 'of independent interest'. It is formalised in a weaker form (continuous data, test_fun_at predicates, an intermediate predicate), so it is FIX_THEN_KEEP and should become a root displayed in Paper_Readings. The fix needs new mathematics in one place only: the localisation doubling_localised_maximiser_soft, which relies on uniform moduli that usc data lack. The rest of its chain uses continuity only via usc_eps_of_continuous and needs a mechanical cu/cw → usc change. That chain is comparison_from_localised_maximiser_gen/_soft, comparison_soft_off_diagonal, comparison_soft_complete and theta_gap_preserved. If D3 instead unifies the localisation with comparison_two_domain, those helpers become DELETE. If the fix fails, keep the current proof, honestly renamed, rather than delete it. Its immediate consequence, the Dirichlet comparison/uniqueness on an arbitrary compact K (viscosity_uniqueness_compact), should stay as a corollary of at most 10 lines restated for usc/lsc data. It is the entry point a user would look up.

A second, smaller keep: local uniform convergence of the sup-convolution for continuous u (supconv_uniform_upper with its helper supconv_sandwich). It is standard Sup_Convolution API, absent from HOL and the AFP. Move it to SOVA.Sup_Convolution, generalised and reproved via the live supconv_le_of_local_bound_usc (KEEP_API).

Everything else is dead weight:
- abandoned routes: exact-jet closing, quadratic or naive doubling, the diagonal p=0 explorations, smooth comparison on the ball, and the refuted comparison_principle locale;
- twins of live code: the VCI scaling lemmas and visc_subsol_scaled_strict;
- continuous-data copies of usc lemmas: sup-convolution attainment and supconv_le_of_local_bound, whose general form is CIS/DoV;
- re-exports and special cases of the live Theorem 1.1 clauses: iexit_val_*, theorem_1_1_uniqueness_general, and the ball fragment on the deleted market layer.

Envelope ellipticity and the explicit ball solution are DELETE with medium confidence. They would only be worth redoing generically, under G-C/G-G or as a SOVA 'classical solutions are viscosity solutions' lemma.

One gap noticed: Remark 1.1(b), 'F is geometric', is not formalised; only an orphan heading remains at VCI:141. It is a candidate new root, built from the live ell_op_scaleR_p/ell_op_scaleR_matrix and not from the VCI twins.

Line totals (extent to the next command):
- FIX_THEN_KEEP ≈ 728: 4.2(a) 173 plus helpers 555, of which 180 are rewritten.
- KEEP_API ≈ 84: about 30 after the move.
- COROLLARY ≈ 1214: about 10–20 kept, as the restated Dirichlet corollary plus an inlined supersol_jet_extend; the rest deleted.
- DELETE ≈ 1213.
- Total ≈ 3240.

Hazards when deleting:
- VCI's global 'declare transpose_matrix_vector [simp del]';
- Ball_Solution's live feasible_diag_bound and feasible_offdiag_abs_le, which must move first;
- the Ball_Solution imports of Exit_Class and Value_Function_Uniqueness;
- the predicate definitions max_principle_boundary(_raw), SOVA max_principle_boundary_gen and the OEC max_principle_* corollaries, which go once 4.2(a) is stated directly.

Prose naming deleted lemmas also needs correcting: the CP 'Map', CP:1266-74 and Viscosity_Definitions:127-143.

**Verifier.** I confirm 34 of the classifier's 36 items; two verdicts change (listed in the changes). Every listed line is covered by exactly one item.

**The core keep is right.** max_principle_boundary_holds is the paper's Theorem 4.2(a), which the paper itself calls 'of independent interest'. It is formalised in a weaker form (continuous data, test_fun_at predicates), so FIX_THEN_KEEP. viscosity_uniqueness_compact stays as a short corollary of it. theta_gap_preserved and comparison_soft_complete stay as its helpers.

**The deletions hold up.** I tried to make an independent-interest case for each DELETE and COROLLARY and none survives:
- None is a paper statement. I checked Lemma 3.1, Example 3.1 and Sections 4–5. In particular, envelope ellipticity is not stated in the paper, and F_* = F holds everywhere by the formalised Lemma 3.1.
- None is a classical theorem absent from HOL/AFP that is not already live elsewhere in better form.
- The ball strand adds no faithfulness evidence. Theorem 1.1 together with Example 3.1 already implies that ball_v solves the equation, since the formal F is the one both are proved for.
- The VCI scaling lemmas and visc_subsol_scaled_strict are verbatim twins of live code.
- The continuous sup-convolution attainment copies and supconv_le_of_local_bound are special cases of the usc lemmas (CIS.supconv_attained_usc, DoV.supconv_le_of_local_bound_usc). I checked the gap implication.
- env_strict_contradiction_of_limits and the _of_shifts and exact-jet lemmas are special cases of the live _of_nearby, _of_shifted_limits and _onesided lemmas.

**Notes for executing the plan (no verdict change):**

1. **A simpler fix for doubling_localised_maximiser_soft.** The proof needs only two local changes, not sequences in ε. The rest of the parameter threading stays.
   - Replace uniform_modulus_on_compact and doubling_maximiser_far_from_boundary by one compactness lemma for usc u and lsc w on compact K. It says: if the maximum of θu−w over the boundary is at most m < M, there is δ > 0 such that |p−q| ≤ δ and θu(p)−w(q) ≥ M imply that p and q are more than δ from the boundary. The proof is limsup θu(p_j) − liminf w(q_j) ≤ (θu−w)(p0) ≤ m.
   - Replace supconv_uniform_upper in the value transfer by attainment points: supconv_attained_usc plus supconv_attain_radius.
   - This raises my confidence in that item's FIX_THEN_KEEP to medium.

2. **Precision in the 4.2(a) fix.**
   - The classifier asks for u usc and w lsc *relative to K*. But usc_extend_const_below (DoV:2666) assumes usc on the whole space. Either state 4.2(a) with global usc and lsc, as comparison_two_domain does, or generalise usc_extend_const_below to relative usc; its proof only reads u on K.
   - Paper Def. 3.1 makes u bounded, so the bound hypotheses are faithful.

3. **The 'needs' lists clash with the sova_doubling cluster.** That cluster deletes compact_frontier_nonempty, theta_exists_aux and doubling_upper_bound_exists.
   - compact_frontier_nonempty should be replaced by HOL frontier_eq_empty with not_bounded_UNIV, not kept.
   - theta_exists_aux should be inlined.
   - With my first change, cball_prod_subset_of_far_from_boundary is no longer needed either.

## cps_tightness_qv

**Classifier summary.** What is of independent interest in this cluster:

1. **Kolmogorov-Chentsov tightness criterion** (tight_on_set_path_laws_vec and its quantitative helper; 227 lines, FIX_THEN_KEEP).
   - The CPS ROOT promises it, and it is not in the distribution or the AFP.
   - It needs a parameter instead of the paper constant 8C², a normed heine_borel codomain instead of real^'m, and a form for laws with an almost-sure start, so the live pair-class tightness can become an instance.
   - The convergent-subsequence form stays as a corollary.
2. **Fourth-moment bound for unbounded continuous L² martingales** (fourth_moment_L2; 351 lines with its three helpers, FIX_THEN_KEEP).
   - It is the paper's moment bound, with 8C², and the live library has only the bounded case.
   - The paper session re-proves it for class members, so it can become live.
   - Its Bochner forms stay as corollaries.
3. **Domination of finite Borel measures by continuous [0,1]-valued test functions** (metric_measure_mono_bounded_cts; 180 lines, KEEP_ROOT).
   - It is general measure theory, not in the AFP, and the live eqI lemma becomes a 10-line corollary of it.
4. **Smaller keepers.**
   - The metric chaining modulus (dyadic_pair_modulus; 75 lines, FIX_THEN_KEEP) is the general form of a live inline computation.
   - The boundedness-free polarisation core (Xpol/Apol/polarised; 132 lines, FIX_THEN_KEEP) replaces the ~180-line inline copy in the live qvmat_eq_A_localised.
   - Measurability and basic API facts of qvps, qvmat, qvp_good and finite-dimensional marginals (51 lines, KEEP_API).
5. **Borderline:** the diagonal/consistent extraction (252 lines, FIX_THEN_KEEP with low confidence). It is relative compactness on C([0,∞)) read horizon by horizon, which is how the paper states Lemma 2.2. Keep it only if the owner wants a C([0,∞)) compactness API.

What is not of independent interest:
- **Continuous-modification chain** (dyadic_ext/lim_*): a twin of AFP Kolmogorov_Chentsov.continuous_modification, with its deterministic half in HOL-Analysis.
- **Projective-limit wrapper:** an instance of HOL-Probability's Daniell-Kolmogorov theorem that lands on the product σ-algebra, on an abandoned route superseded by the live finite-horizon extension.
- **Scalar tightness twins.**
- **Twins of live or library lemmas:** continuous_map_real_diff (library continuous_map_diff), weak_conv_on_prob_limit, dyadic_qsum_eq_qvar.
- **flip_measurable.**
- **Bounded-matrix QV locale shell and its bounded qvmat_eq_A:** a weaker twin of the live localised theorem, which should move into CPS Adapted_Quadratic_Variation.

Line totals (statement extents, approximate):

| Verdict | Lines |
|---|---|
| DELETE | ≈1 640 |
| FIX_THEN_KEEP | ≈1 037 |
| COROLLARY | ≈266 (to shrink to ≈40) |
| KEEP_ROOT | 180 |
| KEEP_API | 51 |
| KEEP_EVIDENCE | 0 |

**Verifier.** The classification is sound. I confirm 24 of the 26 items, and every listed line is covered by exactly one item. I checked each against the source, HOL, the AFP and the paper.

**Confirmed on evidence**
- **Scalar tightness twins:** the vector proof does not use them.
- **The dyadic_ext/lim_* chain and lim_continuous_modification:** an instance of AFP `Kolmogorov_Chentsov`, which works for any `'b::polish_space` with `E dist^a <= C dist^(1+b)`. None of these names is used outside Path_Tightness. `path_law_limit_moment_bound`, `lim_coordinate_moment_bound` and `projective_limit_of_consistent_path_laws` have no user at all, so the route was never assembled.
- **Other twins of live or library code:**
  - `continuous_map_real_diff` is HOL's `Abstract_Limits.continuous_map_diff`.
  - `weak_conv_on_prob_limit` is the same as `Path_Space.weak_conv_on_prob_space`.
  - `dyadic_bad_event_sets_strict` is the strict twin of the live `dyadic_bad_event_sets`.
- **`tight_on_set_path_laws_vec`:**
  - Neither HOL nor the AFP has a tightness criterion for path laws; AFP `tight_on_set` occurs only in Prokhorov and Riesz.
  - It is the only thing that delivers the CPS ROOT's promise of 'tightness from increment moments'. The live route re-does the argument natively in `Exit_Class_Tightness`.
- **`fourth_moment_L2`:** `Exit_Class_Limits.exit_class_stopped_fourth_moment` and `exit_class_fourth_moment` repeat its localisation-plus-Fatou argument for class members. `Aad` follows from `mgX` and `mgZ`, exactly as `Aadapt` does in `compensator_cond_increment`. `stopped_covariation` is `compensator_cond_increment` applied to the stopped pair.
- **`metric_measure_mono_bounded_cts`:** the only AFP relative is `Riesz_Representation.finite_tight_measure_eq`, which is equality-only and needs a locally compact space. The live `metric_measure_eqI_bounded_cts` follows from it in about 10 lines.
- **`dyadic_pair_modulus`:** it is the metric, factor-`E` form of block K inside `Modulus_Tails.modulus_of_good_path`.
- **`qvps_mono_lip` and `qvps_continuous`:** they follow from `qvsa_eq_qvps`, which depends only on the live `qvps_as_Sup`, together with `qvsa_mono`, `qvsa_lip` and `qvsa_continuous`.
- **The bounded-matrix locale:** `qvmat_eq_A_localised` has the same hypotheses without `bnd`/`R`. The only gaps are AE versus pointwise hypotheses and a bounded start.

**Two changes**
1. **Diagonal and consistent extraction:** DELETE, not FIX_THEN_KEEP. It is only meaningful inside the abandoned projective-limit route, and CPS deliberately puts no weak-convergence topology on C([0,inf)), so it has no API to join.
2. **`dyadic_qsum_eq_qvar`:** KEEP_API, not DELETE. It is the general form; the live `dyadic_qsum_eq_grid` is its locale-scoped instance and is the one to delete.

**Notes for neighbouring clusters (no verdict change here)**
- **`marginal_map_measurable` (KEEP_API):** consider splitting out its inner one-time evaluation fact as `Path_Space.path_eval_measurable`. The paper session re-derives that fact inline at 4 sites (`continuous_map_measurable[OF continuous_map_path_eval]`).
- **`Pair_Path_Space.path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market`** is dead and outside this cluster. It combines `fourth_moment_L2` with `tight_on_set_path_laws_vec`, which gives Lemma 2.2 at a fixed horizon for any per-coordinate L2 martingale whose compensator grows at rate at most C. If both are kept, this is the natural headline, and it would be the better root for the CPS/path-toolkit owner.

## ctm

**Classifier summary.** Of independent interest in this cluster:
(a) Textbook entry points of results CTM already proves in a more general form. doob_maximal_inequality' is the textbook Doob weak maximal inequality, and nothing in HOL or the AFP has Doob's maximal inequality. optional_sampling is continuous-time optional sampling, E Z(u/\tau) = E Z 0. stopped_expectation is the discrete fair-game identity. All three should be kept as short corollaries of their live set-integral versions and declared roots; their old proof helpers (inc_zero, simple_optional_sampling, dyadic_expectation) go.
(b) General transfer-calculus theorems that are not in the AFP. martingale_coarser_filtration (tower property) needs one fix: a sigma-finite rather than a finite G-filtration. martingale_uniform_measure (conditioning on an F_0 event) needs t0 and the index type generalised, and keeps its four Bochner-integral helpers for uniform_measure, which HOL lacks.
(c) The moment criterion for uniform integrability (unif_integrable_of_moment_bound with tail_le_moment): a genuine KEEP_ROOT, and the paper's own route into Vitali.
(d) The discrete optional stopping theorem in process form (martingale_stopped) and the stopped energy identity E X^2_{n/\T} = E X_0^2 + E[X]_{n/\T}. They are kept, but must move out of the duplicate locale stopped_sq_int_martingale into Optional_Sampling.nat_stopped_martingale, where martingale_stopped needs only integrability, and into a combined L2 locale. The duplicate Tgt machinery, the twin stopped_integrable and stopped_qvar_expectation_le are deleted. The AFP has only the submartingale form, in Fair_Games_Theorem, which CTM does not import.
(e) Cheap API: qvar_nonneg and qvar_mono, X_measurable, expectation_sq_mono, ess_inf_time_ge_iff_measure, distr_restrict_full, stopped_cont_martingale.stopped_integrable, dtime_eq_dceil (needed for the dyadic de-duplication), and expectation_sq_sampled (energy identity along a partition, kept as a corollary in place of its Time_Discretisation twin).
Not of interest, deleted: Moment_Bounds (whole theory), sq_times_sq, pow4_binomial, the market-layer helpers set_integral_lborel_singleton and set_integral_at_origin, kernel_mix_measurable, martingale_of_cond_increment, the Time_Discretisation re-exports and twins with grid_expected_qvar_indep, integrable_clamp (twin of clamp_integrable), indicator_times_integrable, dceil_grid and maxabs_meas_X.
Tooling artefacts, not dead code: every *.axioms and *.cong fact of a live locale or definition; the measure_pmf.* and measure_spmf.* interpretation copies of live lemmas (indep_vars_cong_sets, vitali_convergence, unif_integrable_limit_integrable); and the collective name dy_floor, whose components are live. These are marked KEEP_API and should be filtered by the dead-code script.
Line totals by verdict, measured as extents to the next command:
- KEEP_ROOT: 81
- FIX_THEN_KEEP: 404. Of these, 235 are the Quadratic_Variation stopped section, expected to shrink to about 150 after the move; 134 are the uniform_measure group; 35 are martingale_coarser_filtration.
- COROLLARY: 104, expected to shrink to about 25 once re-derived.
- KEEP_API: 85 real lines plus 361 lines that are generated or interpretation artefacts of live code.
- DELETE: 318.
- KEEP_EVIDENCE: 0. martingale_coarser_filtration is not needed as evidence, because xclass already uses X's own filtration.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound, and every listed line is covered. Of the 58 items, 57 are confirmed. The 58th is split: martingale_stopped keeps FIX_THEN_KEEP, and the helper grouped with it, stopped_incr_integrable, should be DELETE (see the change).

Checks behind the confirmed verdicts:

**Not in the AFP or HOL (grep of /opt/afp/thys and the HOL sources):**
- No Doob maximal inequality. Doob_Convergence has only upcrossings, so doob_maximal_inequality' stays as a short corollary and root.
- Uniform integrability appears nowhere, so the de la Vallee-Poussin p-moment criterion unif_integrable_of_moment_bound with tail_le_moment is a genuine KEEP_ROOT.
- No lemma moves a martingale to a smaller filtration (martingale_coarser_filtration).
- HOL has only nn_integral_uniform_measure, not the Bochner-integral facts for uniform_measure, so the four helpers of martingale_uniform_measure are genuinely missing.

**martingale_stopped:** the AFP has only the submartingale form (Fair_Games_Theorem.stopped_process_submartingale). verification_results.md re-derived the martingale form in about 16 lines, but only from copied sources, because CTM does not depend on Fair_Games_Theorem. Keeping a local version on nat_stopped_martingale is therefore justified. That locale already has a subsection titled 'The stopped process is itself a martingale' but never states the theorem, which is one more reason to move it there.

**Corollary derivations, read in the source:**
- optional_sampling follows from set_optional_sampling at v = 0, A = space M. meas_v reduces to measurability of Z 0, because tau >= 0 on space M.
- stopped_expectation follows from set_stopped_expectation at A = space M, m = 0.
- expectation_sq_sampled follows from sq_int_martingale_sampled and expectation_sq_qvar.

**Deletions I tried to rescue and could not:**
- Moment_Bounds does not prove Eq. (2.7). It only assembles abstract hypotheses with (t-s) hard-wired. The real (2.7) is Increment_Moments.fourth_moment_bound_bounded.
- grid_expected_qvar_indep is the payoff advertised in the Time_Discretisation header. Still, it is a 3-line consequence of expectation_sq_sampled, which is kept, so DELETE is acceptable. If an entry point is wanted, it could at most be a free-standing corollary of expectation_sq_sampled.
- set_integral_lborel_singleton is the integral companion of HOL's set_borel_integrable_singleton. It is plain Lebesgue theory and part of no CTM constant's interface, so DELETE stands.
- integrable_clamp is a twin of the more general live clamp_integrable.
- martingale_of_cond_increment adds nothing to the AFP's martingale_of_cond_exp_diff_eq_zero plus cond_exp_diff.

**Accepted with low stakes:** sq_int_martingale.X_measurable as KEEP_API. It duplicates the AFP's random_variable rule (premise 0 <= n), and its only named user is the deleted maxabs_meas_X. It mirrors the live X_integrable, so keeping it is harmless.

## market_a

**Classifier summary.** Nothing in market_a is of independent interest. Every item is either a twin of the live class layer, an assumption restated as a theorem, or a vacuous stand-in for stochastic calculus.

The cluster is the first six theories of the market layer: Volatile_Market, Ito_Market, Brownian_Market, Optimal_Exit_Time, Brownian_Optimal_Boundary and Value_Function_Market, 1 914 lines.

The candidates I checked against the owner's independent-interest criterion, and why each fails:

- **Definition 1.1 (relative_arbitrage).** It is a paper definition, not a result. The formal version drops the trading strategy and self-financing, which are the substance. It cannot be repaired, because neither Isabelle nor the AFP has an Ito integral. Paper_Readings should say in prose that Definition 1.1 and the FTAP representation T* = v(U mu(0)) are outside the formalisation.
- **ball_relative_arbitrage.** It looks like the "T > v(x0) implies a relative arbitrage exists" half of the T* characterisation. But the stochastic integral is defined to satisfy Ito's formula, so the theorem is pathwise algebra. It is not a statement of this paper either.
- **expected_exit_time_bound, E[tau] <= (r^2-|x0|^2)/(n-k).** This is the expectation form of the upper half of Example 3.1. The paper's Example 3.1 itself is the live deliverable example_3_1_closed_form, reached through exit_class_sq_norm_mean_ge and exit_val_le_ball_bound, a better route that needs no optional sampling. The expectation form here is obtained only by assuming the Dynkin/Ito identity as a locale axiom, so nothing reusable is left. A general version (continuous L^2 martingale with quadratic-variation trace rate >= c) would be new mathematics on top of CTM's optional sampling, not a repair.
- **Ito_Market.** Its only mathematical step is an instance of CTM's stopped_cont_martingale.optional_sampling, which CTM keeps. Z_expectation_const duplicates Increment_Moments.martingale_expectation_eq and AFP martingale.set_integral_eq.
- **val_fn_mono.** Monotonicity of v in K is genuinely natural, and the third case of Lemma 5.3 uses it. But this proof is for the non-faithful val_fn and transfers nothing. A class-level version should be recorded in OPEN_ITEMS.md with Section 5, not kept from here.
- **Small matrix lemmas.** trace_bound_pointwise is a corollary of SMS trace_ge_dim and a twin of feasible_trace_lb. trace_le_eigen_ub is a trivial eigen_ub fact whose live special case is feasible_trace_le. Both are about paper constants, so KEEP_API cannot apply. Phase 4 may use them as templates if it consolidates the eigen_lb/eigen_ub bounds in Curvature_Operator.

There is one thing the layer gestures at that would be of interest, and it is proved nowhere: a transfer theorem saying that every continuous martingale on any probability space satisfying the paper's constraints has ess inf tau_K <= v(x). That would be new work on the class layer and the paper_class_lift bridge, not a reuse of this code.

**Line totals (statement extents):**

| verdict | lines | items |
|---|---:|---:|
| DELETE | ≈1 550 | 17 |
| COROLLARY (delete; general result is trace_ge_dim) | 13 | 1 (trace_bound_pointwise) |
| KEEP_ROOT, KEEP_API, KEEP_EVIDENCE, FIX_THEN_KEEP | 0 | 0 |

The six theories (1 914 lines with prose) can go whole.

**Deletion mechanics:**

- Value_Function_Uniqueness imports Value_Function_Market. Exit_Time_Semicontinuity (market_b) imports Value_Function_Market and Path_Tightness_Market. Path_Tightness_Market imports Ito_Market.
- The class chain reaches Wiener_Measure.Continuous_Brownian_Motion only through Brownian_Market, so Exit_Class_Witness must import it directly first.
- Antiquotations naming these theories exist at Exit_Class_Witness.thy:32 and Exit_Time_Semicontinuity.thy:23 and :1896.
- Prose in the Wiener_Measure and CTM library theories names these locales (reader finding) and should be cleaned up in the same pass.
- None of these theories makes a global simp, measurable or declare change, so deleting them cannot change simp behaviour elsewhere.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound for 16 of 18 items. I checked each against the source and the paper: Definition 1.1 and ball_relative_arbitrage are vacuous, since sint is defined to satisfy Ito's formula; optimal_ball_market axiomatises its key inequality; val_fn is not the paper's v; Brownian_Market and Brownian_Optimal_Boundary only inhabit deleted locales; the ito_stopped_market and ito_const_horizon_market locales are uninterpreted, and their content duplicates library facts. Deleting these, and recording class-level monotonicity of v in K in OPEN_ITEMS, is right.

One correction. The classifier dismissed the expected-exit-time estimate as 'no reusable content', but it is a general inequality the paper itself relies on. The paper's proof of Example 3.1 displays the expectation chain, and Section 3 cites 'By Example 3.1, E^y[tau] < infinity'. The live class route proves only the ess-inf bound, and the estimate is absent from HOL and the AFP. CTM advertises optional sampling and exit times, and already has etime_stopping_time and etime_stays_in_cball, written for exactly this use, but no theorem about an exit-time moment.

So four core lemmas of Volatile_Market 159-342 and the optional-sampling step of Ito_Market 136-218 should become FIX_THEN_KEEP. They should be re-proved, without the market locales, as one general CTM theorem: if Y - A is a continuous martingale, Y <= R and A grows at rate >= c up to tau, then E[tau] <= (R - E[Z_0])/c. A short class-level corollary E_Q[iexit K] <= (r^2-|x|^2)/(n-k) goes in Exit_Class_Witness. x0_in_K, expected_stopped_time_ball_v, the locales, ito_Z and Z_zero_expectation stay DELETE.

Two minor remarks, neither a verdict change. trace_le_eigen_ub is strictly more general than the live feasible_trace_le, since it needs neither psd nor symmetry, so when phase 4 consolidates the eigen_ub bounds it should keep the general statement and derive the feasible version from it. borel_measurable_ball_v is a one-line corollary of continuous_on_ball_v. Every listed line is covered.

## market_b

**Classifier summary.** I re-checked all 43 listed items against the independent-interest criterion. Only one item, eigen_ub_diag, has value outside the market layer.

The cluster is the tail of the market layer: Path_Tightness_Market plus the 2,634-line Exit_Time_Semicontinuity law-closure route. Every statement is quantified over `sufficiently_volatile_market`, the third value function `stopped_val_fn`, or `mkt_path_laws`/`mkt_law_closure`. None of these is the paper's object. They use the non-convex (1.4) constraint, imposed only up to a locale time tau, on a fixed sample space, with ess_inf of tau instead of tau_K. The prose that calls them 'the paper's class (1.7)' is false. So no item is a faithful paper statement, and none qualifies as evidence.

Where the mathematics is general, the general form already exists elsewhere, mostly live:
- Tightness (Lemma 2.2 core): Pair_Path_Space.path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market and CPS path_laws_convergent_subsequence_vec.
- Weak-limit martingale property and the continuous-tests-to-past-events upgrade: Pair_Path_Laws.martingale_F_limit and martingale_event_F_limit, for any continuous F on a Polish x Banach pair path.
- Conditional increment/compensator identity: CTM cond_exp_increment_sq and CPS compensator_cond_increment.
- Bounded test against a martingale: CTM martingale_bounded_test.
- Closed-set Portmanteau: Path_Space.weak_conv_closed_full_measure.
- Berge via sequential compactness: Pair_Path_Space.vshift_sup_usc_of_seq_compact and Berge.seq_compact_closure_of.

The market items are wrappers or proof-level twins of those general facts and of the live class layer: exit_class_convergent_subsequence, exit_class_pshift, exit_val_usc, exit_class_nonempty, exit_val_le_ball_bound and exit_class_martingale_event_limit.

**Kept: eigen_ub_diag (FIX_THEN_KEEP, 12 lines).** `eigen_ub a L ==> a$l$l <= L` without psd is the general form of three live lemmas: Exit_Class.psd_eigen_ub_diag(2), Ball_Solution.feasible_diag_bound(2) and Value_Function_Euler_Construction.sconstraint_diag_le. Move it beside eigen_ub_def in Curvature_Operator (future Relative_Arbitrage_Equation) and make those three one-line instances.

**Deleted: 14 items, about 2,750 lines.** Path_Tightness_Market.market_path_laws_convergent_subsequence is 321 lines; the Exit_Time_Semicontinuity items are about 2,428.

**Library facts this deletion orphans.** Each has its only user in this cluster and should be judged on its own merits in its own cluster. They do not keep anything here alive:
- Path_Tightness.metric_measure_mono_bounded_cts (cps_tightness_qv);
- Berge.seq_compact_closure_of (sms_semic);
- Path_Space.weak_conv_closed_full_measure, Path_Exit_Times.confined_paths_def, closedin_confined_paths, martingale_test_functional_cont and covariation_test_functional_cont (wm_cps_small);
- Integrability_Criteria.set_integral_at_origin (ctm);
- Pair_Path_Space.path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market (toolkit).

**Prerequisites before the deletion:**
- Delete Exit_Class_Limits.stopped_market_acov_leaves_sconstraint, the only external unfolding of stopped_market_def.
- Drop Exit_Time_Semicontinuity from the imports of Exit_Class_Limits and Value_Function_Uniqueness.
- Add Wiener_Measure.Continuous_Brownian_Motion to the imports of Exit_Class_Witness, which today reaches it only through the market chain.

Totals: FIX_THEN_KEEP 12 lines; DELETE about 2,749 lines; KEEP_ROOT, KEEP_API, KEEP_EVIDENCE and COROLLARY 0.

**Verifier.** I confirm all 15 classifier items, which cover the 44 listed lines. None needs a different verdict. (The classifier's summary says "43 items", but the item list it gives covers all 44 lines.) For each DELETE I tried to argue independent interest, and for the one keep I tried to refute it. Neither attempt succeeded. The evidence follows.

**eigen_ub_diag: FIX_THEN_KEEP stands.**
- I read the source. `eigen_ub a L ==> a$l$l <= L` has no psd hypothesis, so it is strictly more general than the three live lemmas:
  - Exit_Class.psd_eigen_ub_diag(2), whose proof of (2) never uses psd;
  - Ball_Solution.feasible_diag_bound(2);
  - Value_Function_Euler_Construction.sconstraint_diag_le.
- None of these three appears in any dead list. Each re-proves the axis quadratic-form step inline.
- It is not a COROLLARY, because it generalises the live lemmas rather than specialising them.
- KEEP_API does not apply. eigen_ub is defined in Curvature_Operator, a paper theory. psd lives in SMS Symmetric_Spectral, but eigen_ub does not, and SMS Matrix_Algebra only mentions eigen_ub in prose (line 604).
- The proposed home beside eigen_ub_def, plus making the three live lemmas one-line instances, is right.

**Path_Tightness_Market.market_path_laws_convergent_subsequence: DELETE stands.**
- Lines 59-102 build the good event and restrict to it.
- Lines 229-303 (the Arate' block) are pointwise compensator bounds.
- The result then comes from a single call to Pair_Path_Space.path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market.
- It is not the paper's Lemma 2.2, which covers any bounded S. It assumes `K <= cball 0 r`, stopping and the (1.4) constraint only up to tau. Exit_Class_Tightness.exit_class_convergent_subsequence is live.

**mkt_* definitions, closure, Berge usc and nonemptiness: DELETE stands.**
- Every proof is an instantiation of a library lemma: seq_compact_closure_of, weak_conv_on_prob_limit, vshift_sup_usc_of_seq_compact or metrizable_weak_conv_path_topology.
- mkt_path_laws_nonempty depends on the stopped-only constraint. Its witness is a constant process whose acov is mat 1 only at s = 0. It also repeats psd/eigen_lb/eigen_ub of mat 1 verbatim (reader finding at :344-360).

**Event upgrade (mkt_law_closure_martingale_event / covariation_event).** This was the strongest candidate. Reader finding RA-market#2 says the "continuous tests imply past events" upgrade should be one generic path-space lemma. But these items are not that lemma: their statements are tied to mkt_law_closure with an rclamp at 2r. The general mathematics already exists elsewhere:
- Pair_Path_Laws.martingale_event_F_limit (live), stated for any continuous F on a Polish x Banach pair path with a second-moment bound;
- metric_measure_eqI_bounded_cts (live);
- metric_measure_mono_bounded_cts (judged in cps_tightness_qv).

Any generic upgrade lemma should be extracted from the live exit_class_martingale_event_limit, which contains the same push/density block at :400-445, not from here.

**mkt_law_closure_increment_event / sq_increment_event: DELETE stands.** Both are corollaries feeding a "canonical-market construction" that does not exist, and nothing consumes them. They prove only the martingale property and a diagonal covariation bound ≤ L for weak limits of the non-convex family. That is not Lemma 2.3: convex-hull membership is never established.

**stopped_market, stopped_val_fn and clause_one_*: DELETE stands. Nothing here is faithfulness evidence.**
- Nothing in either theory mentions xval, exit_val, exit_class or xclass.
- stopped_val_fn is bounded only by a law-level sup over mkt_law_closure, never by v.
- The only external user of stopped_market_def is Exit_Class_Limits.stopped_market_acov_leaves_sconstraint. It is a one-line definition unfolding, listed as dead in class_dp, and cannot serve as evidence either.

**witness_value_le_ball_v: DELETE stands.** It is a 19-line wrapper, ess_inf ≤ expectation ≤ ball_v, over Volatile_Market.expected_exit_time_bound, which belongs to market_a. The market-form Example 3.1 bound has a live twin, exit_val_le_ball_bound.

**ball_v_le: DELETE stands (medium confidence).** ball_v_def and ball_v_nonneg are themselves dead (operator cluster), and Curvature_Operator is a paper theory, so KEEP_API cannot apply. If the operator cluster keeps ball_v, ball_v_le could come back there as a 2-line lemma, but nothing needs it.

**Other checks.**
- Relative_Arbitrage/ROOT advertises nothing from the market layer.
- Every general ingredient named by the classifier is either live (martingale_event_F_limit, martingale_F_limit, vshift_sup_usc_of_seq_compact, exit_class_*) or is listed for judgment in its own cluster (seq_compact_closure_of, weak_conv_closed_full_measure, confined_paths, the test-functional continuity lemmas, set_integral_at_origin, path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market, psd_diag_nonneg).

**One caveat for executing the eigen_ub_diag fix.** Pair_Path_Space.psd_diag_nonneg is dead today (toolkit cluster). Re-deriving the nonnegativity halves of the live lemmas from it would make it live, so the toolkit/SMS verdict should account for that.

**Minor clean-up.** Exit_Time_Semicontinuity lines 24-43 hold three empty subsection headers left over from the moved exit-time block. They vanish with the theory, but no prose elsewhere should keep pointing to them. Berge.thy:130 still cites 'Exit_Time_Semicontinuity.etime_shift_box'.

About 88% of the dead lines should stay; only about 230 lines are dead weight.

**Paper statements (KEEP_ROOT):**
- **Lemma 2.1, exact (no closure):** `lemma_2_1_exact` with its hypersimplex chain and the two `Constraint_Set_Convexity` generators. The working tree's `Paper_Readings.lemma_2_1` already cites it.
- **Lemma 3.1 in full:** the formula for F^*(0,M), Eq. (3.6) (`eq36` and its about 520-line lower/upper-bound chain in `Operator_Formula`), plus the off-origin clause `ell_op_envelopes_eq_off_zero`. They are dead only because Section 4 needs just F^*(0,0) < 1. Recommendation: assemble these with the live `ell_op_lsc_at_zero` into one `lemma_3_1`, displayed in `Paper_Readings`.

**Named classical theorems (FIX_THEN_KEEP):**
- **Poincare separation:** promised by Symmetric_Matrix_Spectra's ROOT and theory header but absent there; the AFP has interlacing only for complex Jordan_Normal_Form matrices. Generalise it to two symmetric matrices that agree as quadratic forms on a subspace of codimension r, move it to SMS, and keep a 3-line M_p instance.
- **Courant-Fischer:** `eigen_lb_iff_eigval_ge` with the threshold generalised from 1 to any c, moved to SMS next to `eigval_ge_of_subspace`. The c = 1 instance stays as faithfulness evidence.
- **Hypersimplex decomposition:** stated generically ({c in [0,1]^B, sum c = m} is the convex hull of the indicators of m-subsets), next to `Ky_Fan.two_fractional`. This also removes the global constant named `shift`.
- **Theorem 4.2(a), maximum principle** (`max_principle_boundary_def`, per D3): restated as a theorem with the paper's usc/lsc and env2 hypotheses. Its pointwise-boundary consequences `max_principle_le` and `uniqueness_from_max_principle` stay as short corollaries.
- **Local and global touching:** `visc_subsol_imp_env`, `visc_supersol_imp_env` and `visc_subsol_env_local` are generic viscosity facts. Restate them in `SOVA.Viscosity_Solutions`, which the owner keeps and whose ROOT promises generic predicates.
- **`ell_op_eval` (low confidence):** generalised to F(p, -cI) = c(n-k)/2; it is the operator side of Example 3.1.

**Faithfulness evidence:** `feasible_iff_eigval`, extended with eigen_ub <-> lambda_1 <= L so that the displayed set is the feasible set of Eq. (1.9) verbatim.

**Library API:** `rank1proj_scaleR`, if `rank1proj` moves to SMS; drop its needless p != 0 hypothesis.

**Not of independent interest (DELETE):**
- the `ball_v` constant and its lemmas, unless the comparison cluster keeps a ball corollary phrased through `ball_v`;
- the psd_diff corollaries;
- the clone `ell_op_scaleR_dir` (live `ell_op_scale` has no extra hypotheses) and `Mp_scaleR`;
- the trivial restatements `ell_op_lsc_at_zero_iff` and `comparison_from_max_principle`;
- the unused predicate variants `visc_supersol`, `visc_sol` and `visc_sol_env`;
- `max_principle_boundary_raw`, which exists only for its own trivial refutation;
- the five `_eq_gen` bridges.

**Cross-cluster needs.** The kept items depend on these dead facts elsewhere:
- SMS: `two_fractional`, `eigenbasis_containing_eigenvector`, `exists_top_eigenvector`, `dim_inter_ge`, `quadform_ge_on_span_threshold`, `quadform_le_eigval_1`, `eigval_antimono`, `possum_eq_sum_pos`, `kyfan_minus_possum`, `trace_ge_dim`, `neg_half_trace_ball_op`;
- SOVA: `test_fun_at_quartic_shift` and the generic definitions in `Viscosity_Solutions`;

**Notes:**
- Curvature_Operator line numbers are those of HEAD; the working tree is shifted by +19 after the new `ell_op_sym_part`. All other theories are unchanged.
- I wrote only to the shared scratch directory: `work/dead_names.txt` and `work/operator/deps.py`. If another agent already had a `work/dead_names.txt`, I may have overwritten it.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound: I confirm 26 of 28 items and change 2. All 62 listed lines are covered and none is covered twice.

**Changes:**
- **`ell_op_eval` with its two helpers: DELETE.** It is not a paper statement and has no library home. Its only users are deleted in the comparison cluster. It follows from `eq_3_5` (at p != 0) and from the deliverables, and its 'needs' conflict with sms_semic deleting `neg_half_trace_ball_op`.
- **`max_principle_boundary_def`: DELETE.** Theorem 4.2(a) is kept, but as the restated theorem `Comparison_Principle.max_principle_boundary_holds`, which the comparison cluster already marks FIX_THEN_KEEP. The predicate disappears in every branch of the fix.

**Confirmed after trying to refute or rescue each item:**
- Lemma 2.1 chain: KEEP_ROOT. `lemma_2_1_exact` is now cited by the working-tree `Paper_Readings.lemma_2_1`.
- Hypersimplex: FIX_THEN_KEEP. There is nothing on hypersimplices or Birkhoff decompositions in HOL or the AFP; AFP `Integer_Hull` is unrelated.
- Eq. (3.6) and the M_p top-eigenvector chain: KEEP_ROOT. This is the paper's Lemma 3.1.
- `poincare_separation` and `eigen_lb_iff_eigval_ge`: FIX_THEN_KEEP. Both theorems are promised by the SMS ROOT and the `Poincare_Separation` header and are absent there.
- `feasible_iff_eigval`: KEEP_EVIDENCE.
- The two local/global touching items: FIX_THEN_KEEP, moved to SOVA. sova_tools keeps and reworks `Viscosity_Solutions` and itself asks for 'touching on K implies local touching' as API.
- `rank1proj_scaleR`: KEEP_API, on the condition that `rank1proj` moves to SMS.
- The DELETE items stay DELETE. This includes `ball_v`: the comparison cluster deletes every ball corollary, so the classifier's exception does not trigger.

**Corrections that do not change a verdict:**
1. **Hypersimplex fix.** The premise "'a => real is not a real_vector in HOL" is false. HOL-Library `Function_Real_Vectors` has `instance fun :: (type, real_vector) real_vector`, and AFP `Lp` and `Smooth_Manifolds` use it together with HOL-Analysis. The generic lemma can therefore be stated directly as a convex-hull equality: {c in [0,1]^B, c = 0 off B, sum c = m} = convex hull {indicator T | T <= B, card T = m}. `diag_in_convex_hull` then follows via `convex_hull_linear_image`. The weights form remains an option if that import is unwanted in SMS. Note also that sms_semic would move `two_fractional` back to its consumer unless the hypersimplex is made generic, so the home of the generic lemma should be decided together with `two_fractional`.
2. **Courant–Fischer and Poincaré separation.** Both verdicts stand, but "absent from the AFP" should read "absent for `real^'n^'n`". AFP `Two_Hermitian_Results.Cauchy_Eigenvalue_Interlacing` does prove `courant_fischer` and `cauchy_eigval_interlacing`, for complex Hermitian Jordan_Normal_Form matrices.
3. **`visc_subsol_imp_env` reason.** The claim that the live `exit_val_subsol_bc` re-proves it inline is only half right. Its touching set, interior K together with the boundary points where v > 0, is not open, and its boundary case uses v = 0 outside K. Only the interior half could cite the generic lemma. The verdict rests instead on the SOVA ROOT promise and on the standard local/global equivalence, completed by `visc_subsol_env_local` and its supersolution dual, still to be written.
4. **Uniqueness duplicate.** `uniqueness_from_max_principle` (COROLLARY) and `Comparison_Principle.viscosity_uniqueness_compact`, which the comparison cluster turns into the Dirichlet-uniqueness corollary of 4.2(a), are one lemma: keep exactly one. Likewise, `max_principle_le`, generalised to u <= w on the boundary, is the comparison cluster's Dirichlet comparison corollary. Both should cite the restated theorem, not the predicate.

## sms_semic

**Classifier summary.** Of the 1 985 unused lines in this cluster, about 1 530 are worth keeping once the owner's independent-interest test is applied. Line totals by verdict:
- KEEP_ROOT 693
- FIX_THEN_KEEP 533
- KEEP_API 261
- KEEP_EVIDENCE 46
- COROLLARY 186 (about 10 survive)
- DELETE 266

**Of independent interest (roots):**
- eigval_antimono: the eigval are *ordered* eigenvalues.
- eigval_lipschitz: the eigenvalue continuity the SMS ROOT promises.
- The Courant–Fischer / Poincaré machinery: quadform_ge_on_span_threshold and dim_inter_ge.
  - SMS should state the Courant–Fischer characterisation and a paper-free Poincaré separation as roots. Its ROOT advertises both, and today only paper-specific forms exist, in Operator_Formula.
  - Paper_Readings.eigen_lb_reading becomes the c = 1 instance.
- The measurable selection theorem usc_measurable_selection and its greedy-bisection helpers: promised by the ROOT, absent from HOL and the AFP, and the basis of Proposition 2.4's measurable optimiser.

**Natural API of kept constants:**
- kyfan, eigval and possum: kyfan_0, eigval_1, kyfan_eq_sum_eigval, possum_eq_sum_pos, kyfan_minus_possum.
- hrefl_orthogonal.
- psd basics: diag_eq_inner_axis, trace_nonneg_psd, trace_ge_dim.
- Rayleigh/projection companions of live facts: is_proj_rank1, quadform_le_eigval_1.
- neg_matrix_vector: the rectangular twin of the live, square-only matrix_vector_neg_left. Keep this one and redirect.

**Evidence that the working-tree Paper_Readings already reaches:**
- bracket_eq_sum, via eq_3_5. This also makes the whole possum/eigval chain live.
- two_fractional, via lemma_2_1 → lemma_2_1_exact.

**Kept, but in the wrong form:**
- The second-order necessary condition (local_min_hessian_psd): move to SOVA, generalise to euclidean_space, and relate it to second_order_interior_max.
- eigenbasis_containing_eigenvector: generalise to orthonormal families of eigenvectors.
- exists_top_eigenvector: generalise to "eigval i is an eigenvalue".
- usc_extension_bounded: the ROOT promises it. Merge it with SOVA's live usc_extend_const_below as one relative-usc lemma at metric_space.
- lsc_env_eq_self: weaken its continuity hypothesis to pointwise lsc, mirroring usc_env_eq_at.
- compact_space_dense_seq: re-derive from the AFP's compact_space_imp_separable.
- countably_valued_approx: generalise to separable spaces, and fold or generalise limitin_of_dist_half.

**Corollaries:**
- usc_sup_over_compact: keep as a 3–5-line type-class corollary of the live usc_sup_over_compactin.
- quad_diff_bound and psd_diff_limit: special cases of quad_diff_bound_gen and of psd_limit / psd_diff_limit_shifted. Delete.

**Dead weight:**
- Paper ball-candidate helpers: neg_half_trace_ball_op, quadratic_gradient, norm_less_of_ball.
- Trivialities: inner_diff_self_expand, ball_prod_shift_snd, psd_shifted_diff, sup_diff_attained_on_compact.
- Twins of live code: op_matvec and op_transpose (instances of [simp] rules), diag_entry_quadform, and the semiconvex Hessian chain (twin of SOVA's hessian_abs_bound_of_two_sided).
- Re-proofs of HOL facts that only the market layer uses: closure_of_sequential_limit and seq_compact_closure_of.

**Caveat:** the cluster file was computed against the committed Paper_Readings. In the working tree, eq_3_5, lemma_2_1 and eigen_lb_reading already make several of these items live: two_fractional, bracket_eq_sum, the possum/eigval chain and quadform_ge_on_span_threshold.

**Verifier.** I checked all 39 items against the working tree (Phase 1 is committed, so Paper_Readings.eq_3_5, lemma_2_1 and eigen_lb_reading are roots), the paper, HOL and the AFP. I confirm 38 verdicts and change one: is_proj_rank1 goes from KEEP_API to COROLLARY of the live Ky_Fan.onormal_proj. Every listed line is covered. Line numbers have moved by about 28 since the cluster file was made, so I matched items by name.

Checks behind the confirmations:

**Kept items**
- local_min_hessian_psd, FIX_THEN_KEEP confirmed. The AFP's Unconstrained_Optimization/Second_Derivative_Test has the second-order necessary condition only for real => real under C_k_on 2, and HOL has only the first-order has_derivative_local_min. So the multivariate version is absent. Its hypotheses are exactly SOVA's test_fun_at phi g H x (Test_Functions.thy). The natural home is therefore Second_Order_Viscosity_Analysis, stated as 'test_fun_at plus a local minimum gives g x = 0 and psd H'. It is derived from second_order_interior_max through a 'test_fun_at implies Peano expansion' bridge, which SOVA does not have yet.
- usc_extension_bounded, FIX_THEN_KEEP confirmed. The ROOT promises it, and SOVA's usc_extend_const_below (Doubling_Of_Variables) is the narrower live twin: it needs global usc and euclidean_space.
- lsc_env_eq_self, FIX_THEN_KEEP confirmed. No pointwise lsc_env_eq_at exists. It is also derivable from the live lsc_env_ereal plus the AFP's lsc_hull_liminf_at.
- compact_space_dense_seq, FIX_THEN_KEEP confirmed. AFP Standard_Borel_Spaces Set_Based_Metric_Space:1201 compact_space_imp_separable proves the same thing by the same argument. That theory imports Lemmas_StandardBorel, which is already imported.
- usc_measurable_selection, KEEP_ROOT confirmed. Grep finds no measurable selection theorem in HOL or the AFP, and HOL has measurable_Least, as the classifier says.
- countably_valued_approx, FIX_THEN_KEEP confirmed. Grep finds nothing in HOL or the AFP for metric-space rounding or Caratheodory measurability. Its only consumer is the dead kernel route, so low confidence is right.
- quadform_ge_on_span_threshold is already in the closure through Paper_Readings.eigen_lb_reading -> eigen_lb_iff_eigval_ge.
- The AFP has Courant-Fischer and interlacing only for complex JNF matrices (Two_Hermitian_Results). So eigval_antimono, eigval_lipschitz and the planned Courant-Fischer root are absent for real^n^n.
- dim_inter_ge is a two-line consequence of HOL's Vector_Spaces.dim_sums_Int, so it qualifies only as a helper of the planned paper-free Poincare separation. That is how the classifier justified it.
- hrefl_orthogonal: HOL's Cartesian_Space.orthogonal_matrix_mul exists, so the suggested shortening of rotv_orthogonal works.
- neg_matrix_vector: HOL has no negation rule for *v.

**Deleted items**
- closure_of_sequential_limit and seq_compact_closure_of: the content is in HOL's Abstract_Metric_Spaces (closure_of_sequentially, and compact_closure_of_eq_Bolzano_Weierstrass with compactin_sequentially).
- op_matvec and op_transpose: exact instances of the [simp] rules outer_prod_mv and transpose_outer_prod.
- The semiconvex Hessian chain: a twin of SOVA's hessian_abs_bound_of_two_sided.
- neg_half_trace_ball_op: used only by Curvature_Operator.ell_op_eval, feasible_value_ge_one and ell_op_le_one_of_psd_diff. These form the PDE check of the ball candidate. The paper proves Example 3.1 probabilistically, and the live example_3_1_xval route does not need them.
- quad_diff_bound and psd_diff_limit: correctly marked COROLLARY of quad_diff_bound_gen and of psd_limit / psd_diff_limit_shifted. Both have only dead users.

**Placement remarks that do not change verdicts**
- bracket, displayed via eq_3_5, is the paper's operator form L*possum n + (kyfan m - possum m). A library session should arguably not carry it, so the plan should consider moving the bracket definition and bracket_eq_sum to Relative_Arbitrage_Equation.
- diag_eq_inner_axis is a composite of the live inner_axis_one and matrix_vector_axis_one. The live Exit_Class.psd_eigen_ub_diag repeats it inline. Merge all of these with Pair_Path_Space.psd_diag_nonneg into one SMS lemma, psd a ==> 0 <= a$i$i.
- eigval_lipschitz's constant 2i can be tightened to 2i-1. Weyl's inequality becomes a roughly 15-line corollary once the Courant-Fischer root is in SMS.

## sova_doubling

**Classifier summary.** What this cluster contains: about 2 000 lines, nearly all from the quadratic-penalty Theorem 4.2(a) route that was abandoned. The live route uses the _gen (general-penalty) twins of these lemmas.

**Of independent interest:**

1. **KEEP_ROOT, 290 lines, 12 facts.** The quadratic-coupling lemmas that the owner-declared root Crandall_Ishii_Sums.theorem_on_sums_quadratic(_closed) needs through theorem_on_sums_stage:
   - sums_gives_ordering;
   - linear_block_* and sym_block_*;
   - the slice lemmas (penalty_difference_identity*, doubled_slice_numerator_*, doubled_jet_slice_*);
   - tilted_doubled_jet_slices.

   They are typed at 'a::euclidean_space. The live _gen chain is typed real^'n with a matrix Z, so they are not instances of it. Recommendation: move them into a 'quadratic coupling' section of Crandall_Ishii_Sums, their only consumer. That leaves Doubling_Of_Variables holding only the general-penalty toolbox. An optional alternative is to lift the _gen chain to 'a with Z a symmetric linear map; then all of these become instances.
2. **KEEP_API, 52 lines.** The general-penalty doubling basics that the ROOT's 'doubling toolbox' promises: diagonal_max_gen, off_diagonal_gen and penalty_bound_gen. They are cheap, general, and not in HOL or the AFP.
3. **FIX_THEN_KEEP, 46 lines.** doubling_maximiser_exists_gen should be restated for usc u and lsc w (the regularity sub- and supersolutions actually have). That means moving CIS's usc_attains_sup_compact ahead of Doubling_Of_Variables.
4. **COROLLARY kept as short instances, 123 lines shrinking to about 15.** These are the CIS call targets tilted_doubled_hessian_nonpositive and doubled_supconv_jet_exists_shifted, both instances of live _gen lemmas, and doubling_dist_bound, the classical O(1/α) estimate. Re-deriving doubled_supconv_jet_exists_shifted from _gen also frees Theorem_On_Sums.doubled_functional_semiconvex_shifted, doubled_semiconvexity_constant_pos and norm_sq_prod_split.

**Not of interest:**

- **COROLLARY to delete, 333 lines, 12 facts.** Quadratic special cases of kept or live general lemmas: doubling_maximiser_exists, the diagonal and penalty quadratic copies, jet_imp_local_max/min_test, sums_ordering_at_interior_max, sums_psd_at_interior_max, doubled_supconv_jet_exists, doubled_jet_slices_at_max, and norm_sq_prod_split (= norm_prod_sq).
- **DELETE, 1 144 lines, 49 facts:**
  - quadratic twins of live _gen code: shifted_jensen_family, tilted_doubled_psd_ordering, the block_form, transpose, matrix_apply and penalty_gradient copies;
  - re-proofs of library facts: bounded_linear.linear, compact_uniformly_continuous (positive_separation_of_value_gap, uniform_modulus_on_compact), Tietze, continuous_on_compact_bound, frontier_eq_empty;
  - paper-specific glue (θu, supconv(−w), Lipschitz gaps) and arithmetic helpers of the dead Comparison_Principle, Comparison_Localisation and Comparison_Strictness quadratic route.

**Caveat on D3.** Four of the deleted helpers (continuous_extension_bounded, bounded_on_compact, compact_frontier_nonempty, theta_exists_aux) serve only max_principle_boundary_holds. If the comparison cluster keeps that proof as the base of a faithful Theorem 4.2(a), they come back as its private helpers. Each is a library one-liner anyway.

**Totals (extents to the next command):** KEEP_ROOT 290, KEEP_API 52, FIX_THEN_KEEP 46, COROLLARY 456 (123 kept as about 15 lines, 333 deleted), DELETE 1 144, out of 1 988.

Scratch scripts are in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-relative-arbitrage/b298b4d9-283e-5a1e-9ff1-6c1924a698bb/scratchpad/indep/work/ (rdeps_dov.py, closure.py, tot.py). The theory reviewed is /home/user/relative_arbitrage/Second_Order_Viscosity_Analysis/Doubling_Of_Variables.thy.

**Verifier.** I confirm 66 of the 80 facts, which is 38 of the 43 classifier items in full; all 80 listed lines are covered. Two kinds of change are needed.

(a) The general-penalty basics (diagonal_max_gen, off_diagonal_gen, penalty_bound_gen) are worth keeping. KEEP_API does not fit them, because there is no constant, and they are stated for one domain K x K. The paper's Thm 4.2(b) and the live comparison_two_domain double over two sets, so the right verdict is FIX_THEN_KEEP, generalised to K x K'. The same applies to doubling_maximiser_exists_gen, which stays FIX_THEN_KEEP and should get both usc/lsc and K x K'.

(b) The quadratic-coupling lemmas kept as KEEP_ROOT helpers of Crandall–Ishii (sums_gives_ordering, linear/sym_block_*, the slice chain, tilted_doubled_jet_slices) are COROLLARY. Crandall–Ishii uses them only at real^'n, and there each is a one-line instance of the live _gen lemma, given a 12-line exact jet of the quadratic penalty (quad_pen_jet, from penalty_difference_identity). I checked these instances in PIDE against the built SOVA heap; the file is indep/work/sova_doubling_verify/QuadInst.thy. The same file also checks the classifier's claim that doubled_supconv_jet_exists_shifted is a 2-line instance of doubled_supconv_jet_exists_shifted_gen, by '(simp add: convex_on_const)' for the sc hypothesis. penalty_difference_identity stays KEEP_ROOT. The rewiring takes Crandall–Ishii's last callers away from Theorem_On_Sums.sums_matrix_inequality and linear/sym_slice_*, so the sova_sums verifier should re-check those.

Confirmed in detail:
- Every DELETE. Each is a library wrapper (Tietze, continuous_on_compact_bound, compact_uniformly_continuous, frontier_eq_empty plus not_bounded_UNIV, bounded_linear.linear, norm_prod_sq), a quadratic twin of live _gen code, Lipschitz or θu glue from the abandoned 4.2(a) route, or parameter arithmetic.
- Every COROLLARY-delete. jet_imp_local_max_test is not needed for the semijet API, because Test_Functions already has jet_test_fun_at and test_fun_quadratic_dominates.
- doubling_dist_bound kept as a corollary. Its reason overstates it: the classical Crandall–Ishii–Lions Lemma 3.1(i), α‖x̂−ŷ‖² → 0, is Theorem_On_Sums.doubling_penalty_tendsto_zero, which is stronger than this O(1/α) bound. Keep both together, and fix that lemma's prose, which credits 'Lemma 3.1 of LaiShkolnikovSoner'; it is Crandall–Ishii–Lions.
- The D3 caveat on the four max_principle_boundary_holds helpers.

## sova_sums

**Classifier summary.** I classified every item in the sova_sums cluster: 3109 lines, 607 in Theorem_On_Sums and 2502 in Crandall_Ishii_Sums. Nothing in the repository was modified. The re-routes and fixes below are argued from the lemma statements; none was checked in PIDE or a build.

**Totals per verdict:**
- **FIX_THEN_KEEP:** 2147 lines (157 in Theorem_On_Sums, 1990 in Crandall_Ishii_Sums). This is the Crandall-Ishii theorem and the helpers it needs, which inherit its verdict, plus the pieces of CIL Lemma 3.1.
- **KEEP_API:** 32 lines.
- **COROLLARY:** 69 lines, shrinking to about 8 when rewritten.
- **DELETE:** 861 lines (381 in Theorem_On_Sums, 480 in Crandall_Ishii_Sums).
- **KEEP_ROOT:** 0. The Crandall-Ishii theorem is declared a root now, as it stands; it is marked FIX_THEN_KEEP only because its statement can be strengthened.

**Of independent interest, and kept:**
- **`theorem_on_sums_quadratic_closed`**, the classical closed-jet theorem on sums. Neither the distribution nor the AFP has viscosity jets or this theorem. It is weaker than its own proof allows:
  - It lacks the block inequality `h·Xh − g·Yg ≤ α/(1−2λα)·|h−g|²`. At λ = 1/(3α) this is exactly (3.10) of the CIL User's Guide.
  - It covers only the quadratic coupling. The paper (Theorem 4.2) uses ε⁻¹|x−y|⁴, and this formalisation uses the soft penalty.
- **The second-order semijet vocabulary.** `superjet_mono` and the two subset-of-closure lemmas are KEEP_API.
- **Classical sup-convolution facts**, kept as helpers:
  - the optimiser location `x + λp`;
  - the jet transfer to `u`;
  - the semiconvexity lower bound on forms, to be stated for any semiconvex function;
  - `supconv_attained_usc`. It should export the radius and move to `Sup_Convolution`, where it replaces the paper-session attainment twins (about 470 lines).
- **`norm_sq_add_expand` and `norm_sq_diff_expand`**, generalised to `real_inner` and moved to the bottom of the session.
- **`second_order_form_unique`**: uniqueness of the second-order form, kept as an 8-line corollary of `expansion_ray_limit`.
- **The unassembled pieces of CIL Lemma 3.1** (doubling-of-variables limit), the classical companion of the theorem on sums. They are kept only if they are assembled into one statement for a general penalty in `Doubling_Of_Variables`, about 60–100 new lines. Otherwise they are DELETE. Confidence is medium.

**Not of independent interest, deleted:**
- **The staged `theorem_on_sums_quadratic` (297 lines).** It is superseded by the closed form, and its helper `sum_squeeze_pair_fst` goes with it.
- **The quadratic-penalty algebra of `Theorem_On_Sums`:** `sums_matrix_inequality`, `penalty_exact` and its two `penalty_form` lemmas, the slice lemmas, and the doubled-functional semiconvexity lemmas. Each is a twin of a live `_gen` lemma.
  - This departs from plan D1, which kept the quadratic lemmas this theorem needs.
  - Deleting them requires routing `theorem_on_sums_stage` through the live `_gen` chain at `real^'n`. The penalty jet is exact (α·I), so the glue should be about 30–50 lines.
  - Until that re-route is done, these items and the quadratic copies in `Doubling_Of_Variables` stay as helpers.
- **Unused sign-flip and rewrite lemmas, and a trivial test lemma.**
- **Re-proofs of library facts:**
  - `linear_add_scaleR`: use `linear_compose_add` with `linear_scaleR`;
  - `tendsto_of_dist_bound`: use `metric_tendsto_imp_tendsto`;
  - `add_sq_le_double`: one line from `sum_squares_bound`;
  - `expansion_add_centered_sq`: twin of the live `jet_transfer_quadratic`;
  - `inner_fst_pair` and `inner_snd_pair`: use `inner_Pair_0`;
  - the usc toolbox (`usc_open_lt`, `usc_attains_sup_compact`, `usc_form_of_continuous`): twins of `Semicontinuity`. These three need `Second_Order_Viscosity_Analysis` to import `Semicontinuity`, which is cheap after phase 5; if that import is rejected, they stay.

**Out-of-cluster dependency:** `theorem_on_sums_stage` uses `Doubling_Of_Variables.norm_sq_prod_split` directly, so that lemma must stay as well.

**Verifier.** I checked the classification against the source, the paper, HOL and the AFP. It is sound: 34 of 36 items confirmed, 2 changed, and every listed line is covered. I did not build anything or check proofs in PIDE.

**Checks that hold up:**
- **The headline theorem.** Neither HOL nor the AFP has semijets, sup-convolution or a theorem on sums, so keeping `theorem_on_sums_quadratic_closed` as a root is justified.
- **The staged theorem.** `theorem_on_sums_quadratic` follows from the closed form: closure membership gives genuine jets, and pointwise convergence of the forms is uniform on unit vectors in finite dimension. Deleting it is right, and so is deleting `sum_squeeze_pair_fst`, which only it uses.
- **`expansion_add_centered_sq`.** It is a twin of the live `jet_transfer_quadratic` with `f := supconv u eps` and `c := xh`. The pattern matches `slices(2)` once `af` is unfolded.
- **The re-route of `theorem_on_sums_stage` onto the general-penalty (`_gen`) lemmas.** It looks like a drop-in:
  - `Pn = (alpha/2)·‖·‖²`, with `kappa = alpha`;
  - the convexity hypothesis `sc` holds trivially;
  - the penalty's expansion (`Pjet`, with `G = alpha·d`, `Z = alpha *R mat 1`) is exact, via `norm_sq_add_expand`;
  - everything stays at `real^'n`.

  So the conditional deletions of the quadratic algebra in `Theorem_On_Sums` are acceptable.
- **The proposed new conjunct `h·Xh − g·Yg ≤ α/(1−2λα)·|h−g|²`.** It is mathematically correct. The Hessian of the separable part is block diagonal, and it equals the nonpositive form plus `α·[[I,−I],[−I,I]]`.

**Changes:**
1. **`usc_open_lt` and `usc_attains_sup_compact`: DELETE → FIX_THEN_KEEP**, home `Semicontinuity`. They are strictly stronger than the library's `usc_attains_sup_gen`, which needs a bound that is in fact redundant. This is the usc extreme-value theorem, which neither HOL nor the AFP has.
2. **`doubling_ge_diagonal` and `doubling_ring_identity`: → DELETE.** The classifier's own fix inlines them, so they should not sit in a kept item.

**Corrections that do not change a verdict:**
- **`supconv_attained_usc`: the radius fix is unnecessary.** The live `DoV.supconv_attain_radius` already bounds the distance to any optimiser by `sqrt(max 0 (2ε(B − u x)))`. The paper-session twins therefore become corollaries of `supconv_attained_usc` plus that lemma. Move `supconv_attain_radius` to `Sup_Convolution` along with it.
- **The `needs` list for the closed theorem and `theorem_on_sums_stage` is incomplete.** It names only `norm_sq_prod_split`. Until the re-route is done, the stage also needs these dead items:
  - from `Doubling_Of_Variables`: `doubled_supconv_jet_exists_shifted`, `tilted_doubled_jet_slices`, `tilted_doubled_hessian_nonpositive`, `sums_gives_ordering`, `linear_block_fst/snd`, `sym_block_fst/snd`;
  - their quadratic helpers in `Theorem_On_Sums`.
- **One more weakness of the closed theorem.** It assumes a global maximum over `real^'n × real^'n`, with global bounds on `u` and `w`. The classical Crandall–Ishii–Lions Theorem 3.2 is local. A localisation corollary using `usc_extension_bounded` belongs with the classifier's fixes (a) and (c).

## sova_tools

**Classifier summary.** Of independent interest in this cluster, ranked:
- **Minty's theorem** (minty_surjective): a named classical result, not in HOL or the AFP, 4 lines. Make it a root.
- **Canonical semiconvex Alexandrov theorem** (semiconvex_alexandrov): keep it as a 3-line corollary of the live semiconvex_alexandrov_bounded instead of its 64-line copy, and declare it a root.
- **A.e. second-order regularity of the Moreau envelope**: merge moreau_twice_differentiable_AE and moreau_alexandrov_sym_AE (with helper moreau_hessian_psd) into one root theorem; delete the weaker moreau_alexandrov_AE.
- **Standard sup-convolution API**:
  - Lipschitz inheritance and the eps*L^2/2 approximation rate: keep;
  - pointwise convergence supconv_tendsto: keep, but weaken from continuity to upper semicontinuity, which is all the proof uses;
  - supconv_alexandrov: keep as a 1-line corollary placed outside Sup_Convolution, so that theory stops importing Alexandrov/Jensen_Lemma.
- **Test-function closure API** matching the live C2 versions:
  - test_fun_at_affine: generalise to an arbitrary matrix and share helpers with test_fun_C2_affine. It is also needed for the Proposition 5.1 pilot (D4b).
  - test_fun_at_quartic_shift: the paper's own "phi + |.-x|^4" step.
- **soft_grad_nonzero**: the advertised property of the live soft penalty.
- **Faithfulness evidence**: convex_expandable, which shows the uniqueness hypothesis is not vacuous. Move it with expandable to Relative_Arbitrage_Equation and display it in Paper_Readings.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound, with one real error. The soft-penalty doubling lemmas (1427-1572) were classed as dead glue, but four of them are in the proof closure of Theorem 4.2(a). Another cluster now keeps that theorem, so they must stay (change above, conditional on the D3 route). 27 of the 28 items are confirmed, and every listed line is covered.

Checks behind the confirmed verdicts:
- Nothing comparable is in HOL or the AFP. A grep for sup/inf-convolution, Moreau, prox, Minty, subdifferential, semiconvex and viscosity finds nothing. "Alexandrov" and "Rademacher" match only unrelated entries (Rademacher_Series, Farey).
- No item is reachable from Crandall_Ishii_Sums.
- The supconv_tendsto proof uses only the upper half of continuity, so usc is enough.
- supconv_jensen_alexandrov_point is an instance of the live semiconvex_jensen_alexandrov_point.
- perturbed_maximiser_interior is the rho = r instance of perturbed_maximiser_deep_interior.
- semiconvex_alexandrov is subsumed by the live semiconvex_alexandrov_bounded.
- The Rademacher and Alexandrov scaffolding consists of proof-internal ddir/dlim_set facts.
- test_fun_at_quartic_shift is needed by the operator cluster's kept visc_subsol_env_local.
- convex_expandable is the paper's own Remark after Proposition 4.1.

Corrections to reasons and execution hazards that do not change verdicts:
1. **soft_grad_norm_pos (DELETE stays).** It is not consumed only by dead code. The LIVE Comparison_Principle.comparison_2dom_off_diagonal (USED) calls it at line 1646, in a `have cpos` step that is never used afterwards. The same happens at line 1566 in the kept comparison_soft_off_diagonal. facts.tsv misses both uses because unused haves leave no trace in the proof term. Remove both `have cpos` lines together with the lemma, or the build breaks.
2. **test_fun_at_affine (FIX_THEN_KEEP stays).** The claim that the D4b / Proposition 5.1 pilot needs it is wrong. comparison_two_domain takes the env2 (C2) predicates, the live visc_subsol_env_imp_env2 weakens v's property first, and the transport then uses the live test_fun_C2_affine. Keep it as API only. HOL2026's Higher_Differentiability_Multi.hessian_affine_compose_on_C2 (any matrix A) supports the arbitrary-matrix generalisation and may also shorten test_fun_C2_affine.
3. **Generic viscosity definitions.** Their 'needs' lists the four old Viscosity_Definitions *_eq_gen bridges, which the operator cluster DELETEs. The rework needs new bridges, including the env2 variants, not the old ones.
4. **Moreau merge (optional, better fix).** moreau_second_order_taylor and moreau_hessian_symmetric use only 'f has gradient G everywhere and G is differentiable at x'. Stated for a general C1 function, they give the Peano form of the symmetric second derivative (HOL has only clairaut_hessian_symmetric for C2). The Moreau a.e. root then becomes a corollary of 'Lipschitz gradient implies twice differentiable a.e.'.
5. **minty_surjective.** It is an existential repackaging of the live prox_subdiff. KEEP_ROOT is acceptable for the named theorem. Optionally, state the full resolvent identity (x - y in subdiff f y iff y = prox f x), using Alexandrov.subdiff_prox.
