## toolkit

**Classifier summary.** Toolkit cluster: 60 dead facts and definitions, 1 894 lines. Verdict totals: KEEP_ROOT 126, KEEP_API 480, FIX_THEN_KEEP 110, COROLLARY 195, DELETE 983.

Of independent interest (KEEP_ROOT / FIX_THEN_KEEP, 236 lines):
- path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market (56) is the process-level form of the paper's Lemma 2.2 for any bounded S. Laws of continuous L2 martingales with a Lipschitz compensator have a weakly convergent subsequence. It is generic despite its name. Rename it and move it to Continuous_Path_Spaces, where Path_Tightness.thy:2925 already announces it. It drags in the dead CPS tightness chain (path_laws_convergent_subsequence_vec, tight_on_set_path_laws_vec, path_law_holder_ball_bound_vec) and the Stopped_Localization fourth-moment chain, both in cluster cps_tightness_qv.
- pstopped_vimage_pre_sigma (84 with pre_sigma_ofI_le) is the classical fact that the stopped path is F_theta-measurable, one half of Galmarino's test. With its helpers path_stopping_time_cut_eq and pstopped_cut_compose (42) it becomes a Path_Space_Operations deliverable.
- integrable_at_bounded_stopping_time (54) is the measurability and integrability half of optional sampling at a bounded stopping time. It removes the stopped_measurable assumption of CTM's stopped_cont_martingale. Drop its redundant mono/sub hypotheses and move it to CTM with set_martingale_sampling.

KEEP_API (480):
- The general disintegration path_rcd and path_rcd_ksemi (153). The live exit_class_rcd and exit_class_rcd_ksemi should become one-line instances of them (net -150 lines).
- Basic interface of live constants:
  - covariation_class: the intro rule, the X-clause projection, monotonicity in S;
  - psd: diagonal nonnegativity, for SMS;
  - vshift: the path-law bridge, for CPS;
  - pcut/pglue: algebra rules;
  - path_stopping_time: closure under max and shift, to be merged into one comp_ge lemma;
  - pre_sigma_of: the sigma-algebra property, or deleted if bridged to AFP Doob_Convergence pre_sigma;
  - kglue_law': the reassembly identity, and the AE-iff that live code inlines at Dynamic_Programming_Pasting:63-67;
  - padd/pstopped: invariance under the additive glue, cited by live prose (low confidence).

Not of independent interest (COROLLARY + DELETE, 1 178 lines):
- Paper-specific leftovers of the market, Euler and ball chains: acont, Yint, pair_holder_charge_split, euOrth_mset, and the three ball instances of the live quad_*_region lemmas.
- Duplicates of live facts: covariation_class_martingale_compensated, pexit_pglue_split, and pexit_pcut_ge, which the live pexit_pcut and pexit_min_horizon give as an equality.
- The abandoned elementary-conditioning route: martingale_future_of_past, pfut_law_start.
- covariation_val, a value function with only a trivial lemma, which is paper material in a library.
- The abandoned stopping-time conditioning route, about 520 lines: the post-theta increment path pafter with its measurability, reassembly and padd inversion; pafter_vimage_pre_sigma and its helper; rect_vimage_pre_sigma_stopping; set_integral_increment_times_known; and the corollaries integrable_at_path_stopping_time, path_stopping_time_shift_event, pre_sigma_of_Int, exit_component_dyceil_tendsto.

I checked the paper: Prop. 2.4 (DPP at stopping times, plan D4) does not need the pafter route. The live >= half uses the additive glue at a path stopping time, and the <= half (exit_val_cond_time) works at any random time. The pafter deletion is the least certain call in this cluster (medium confidence). It would be restored, about 100 lines, only if a strong Markov property on path space is ever formalised.

Cross-cluster dependencies:
- vshift_path_law needs Path_Exit_Times.etime_shift_superlevel_closed (cluster wm_cps_small).
- covariation_val_def must stay if the class_dp reviewer keeps Exit_Class.exit_val_eq_covariation_val.
- Prose antiquotations that name deleted facts must be rewritten: Dynamic_Programming_Pasting:84 (pexit_pglue_split), Dynamic_Programming_Additive_Glue:179 (pafter_padd), Exit_Class_Limits:20-70 (acont/Yint), and the PST/PLP orphan sections.

**Verifier.** The classification is sound in substance: 35 of 37 verdicts are confirmed, and every listed line is covered. There are two changes.

(a) covariation_val_def / covariation_val_mono_class: DELETE becomes FIX_THEN_KEEP (move to the paper session). The class_dp cluster keeps exit_val_eq_covariation_val, which needs them. They also yield v nondecreasing in L, the provable half of the open conjecture in the paper's Remark 1.1(d).

(b) set_integral_increment_times_known: DELETE becomes FIX_THEN_KEEP (restate abstractly in CTM). Its core is Doob's optional sampling theorem in conditional-expectation form, which nothing else in the repository, HOL or the AFP states. It is also the only consumer of two helpers the classifier keeps: sigma_algebra_pre_sigma_of and integrable_at_bounded_stopping_time.

Corrections to the reasons of confirmed items (verdicts unchanged):
1. path_laws_convergent_subsequence_market: the fix should also drop the hypotheses 0 < gamma < 1/4. gamma does not occur in the conclusion; instantiate it inside the proof (e.g. gamma = 1/8). Stopped_Localization does not import Path_Tightness, so moving the lemma into CPS needs only a theory reordering.
2. integrable_at_bounded_stopping_time: the claimed 'real gap' is mostly already filled. CTM Stopped_Adaptedness.stopped_adapted_of_cont derives the stronger F_v-measurability for continuous paths, and CTM optional_stopping already discharges stopped_measurable through it. The lemma's remaining value is its weaker path hypothesis (convergence along dyadic ceilings, i.e. right-continuity) and its role as helper of change (b). Its measurability argument is also the one inlined in set_martingale_sampling (the 'gm'/'glim' steps), which should cite it.
3. padd_stopping_time / pstopped_padd: the claim that they are 'the conceptual reason the live aglue route works' is false. The live route evaluates theta only on the past p' (Dynamic_Programming_Assembly, pexit_padd_dpp) and uses pcut_padd_before and pcut_padd_section. The prose at Dynamic_Programming_Additive_Glue:179 is stale. They stay KEEP_API only as interaction rules of kept constants, by symmetry with pcut_pglue.
4. covariation_class_mono's proof cites the deleted duplicate covariation_class_martingale_compensated. Rewrite it to cite the live covariation_class_compensated_martingale.
5. euOrth_mset is literally the live euOrth_mset_cond at SF := (λ_. 0).
6. vshift_path_law is a short instance of the live CTM ess_inf_time_distr. KEEP_API is accepted as the process-to-law adapter for CPS's exit-time deliverable, but it drags in the dead 64-line etime_shift_superlevel_closed, which is the complement form of the live open_etime_shift_less.

The pafter deletion is confirmed. No law-level statement about pafter exists, and the D4 pilot (DPP equality at stopping times) uses exit_val_cond_time and the additive glue, not pafter.

## value_function

**Classifier summary.** Nothing in this cluster is a statement of the paper or a classical theorem that would need its own root. The paper's results at stake here, the Section 3.1 subsolution property and the Section 3.2 Cases 1 and 2, are already live in stronger or faithful forms: exit_val_subsol_bc / exit_val_visc_subsol_any, exit_val_supersol_contradiction_case1_lsc, exit_val_case2_at_minimiser, and the region Euler chain. The dead items here are their superseded variants or special cases.

The only paper-free, reusable mathematics is the pair of Brownian fourth moments, bm_coordinate_pow4 and bm_R2_moment. They are live through their named conclusions but sit in the wrong session, so they move to Wiener_Measure.Product_Brownian_Motion next to bm_coordinate_sq and are re-derived from gauss_measure_fourth_moment.

The fixed-time 'Ito-free expansion' (E X_t = x, E[X_t.M X_t] = x.Mx + tr(M E Y_t), E[Y_t]/t in sconstraint) is the theta = t case of the live stopped moments. The generic part worth extracting under G-B (a weighted mean of a closed-convex-set-valued process stays in the set) should come from the live exit_class_Y_stopped_mean_sconstraint, not from this copy.

The ball Euler chain, the plain Case 1 and the tanp/tanSF block are twins of live general versions. The relaxed operator ell_op_s equals ell_op k L 0, so nothing new is lost with it.

Three items are false positives of the closure analysis:
- sbm_xi_mean0 is the unused collective name of a lemma whose named conclusions are live;
- stopped_X_int and stopped_X_mean are conclusions (1)-(2) of the live exit_class_stopped_moments, more general than the live conclusions (3)-(4);
- eulerp.cases/elims/induct/pelims/pinduct are facts the function package generates. Switching `fun` to `primrec` removes them.

Line totals (extent to the next command):
- DELETE ≈ 1 655 lines: relaxed-operator route 114; ball Euler chain 882; plain Case 1 with its helper 444; tanSF 90; visc_supersol_lsc_iff_env 45; tilt_step 76; tanpV 4. The eulerp generated facts cost 0 lines; eulerp itself stays, as primrec.
- COROLLARY (delete; the general result is live) ≈ 493 lines: fixed-time Y lemmas 147; fixed-time X/quadratic lemmas 168; exit_val_visc_subsol 96; tanp block 82.
- FIX_THEN_KEEP: 134 lines (Brownian fourth moments; about 50 after re-deriving from gauss_measure_fourth_moment).
- KEEP_API (false positives, nothing deletable): sbm_xi_mean0 (126 live lines), stopped_X_int/stopped_X_mean (2 conclusion lines of a live lemma).
- KEEP_ROOT and KEEP_EVIDENCE: 0.

One optional new evidence item is suggested under visc_supersol_lsc_iff_env: for u continuous on K, Definition 3.1 reduces to the classical definition, stated with lsc_envK and the env2 predicates.

Required prose fixes on deletion: the Value_Function_Subsolution intro and NOTES_FOR_AUTHORS section 1 (they describe the dead fixed-time route as the proof), Case_1:813-821, Case_2:583-590 and 975, Uniqueness:59 and 104-116, Pair_Path_Laws:3748. Also rename or replace the live ell_op_s_bdd_below once ell_op_s is gone.

**Verifier.** I confirm 14 of the 15 items. I checked each against its source text, the live general version and the libraries.\n\nWhat I checked, item by item:\n- **Fixed-time Y and X/quadratic chain:** these are the theta = t instances of exit_class_Y_stopped_* and exit_class_stopped_moments. A constant time satisfies path_stopping_time trivially. exit_class_X_integrable/_mean also follow from the live exit_class_X_martingale.\n- **ell_op_s:** equals ell_op k L 0 under k < n and L >= 1, by feasible_subset_sconstraint plus sconstraint_orth_feasible at q = 0. exit_val_subsol_quadratic_global (F(0,M) <= 1) is weaker than the live F(p,M) <= 1.\n- **exit_val_visc_subsol:** a verbatim copy of exit_val_visc_subsol_any with an unused x in interior K.\n- **eulerp generated facts:** eulerp is structural on its nat argument, so primrec works.\n- **Brownian fourth moments:** no Brownian motion in HOL or the AFP (Kolmogorov_Chentsov has none). gauss_measure_fourth_moment allows v = 0. prod_sq_le_half_pow4 is in CTM, which Wiener_Measure can import.\n- **sbm_xi_mean0 and stopped_X_int / stopped_X_mean:** conjunct names of live lemmas.\n- **Ball Euler chain:** the region versions instantiate it (R = cball, RO = ball, M1 = M2), using closest_point_self.\n- **Plain Case 1:** follows from case1_lsc via lsc_env <= v and the continuity of phi. Its helper is touching_grad_lt_horizon_gen at W = enn2real o exit_val.\n- **tanp_*:** the P = mat 1 / k = 1 instances of tanpU_*.\n- **tanSF, tanpV, tilt_step:** confirmed unused (grep), or a composition of tilted_local_touching and exit_val_case2_at_minimiser.\n\nThe one change is visc_supersol_lsc_iff_env: DELETE becomes FIX_THEN_KEEP, as evidence. The formal statement asks the subsolution property of v and of the competitor u, not of u^* as Definition 3.1(a) does. Nothing in Paper_Readings proves that this replacement is harmless.\n\nCorrections to the classifier's notes; they do not change any verdict:\n(a) Deleting exit_class_Y_mean_sconstraint also breaks the document antiquotation at Value_Function_Subsolution.thy:1273 ('The weighted analogue of @{thm [source] exit_class_Y_mean_sconstraint}'). This is missing from the classifier's list of prose fixes. The full list of antiquotations naming deleted items: Subsolution:1273, Assembly:16, Case_1:1127 and 1309, Euler:2543, 2703 and 3230, Uniqueness:80.\n(b) The fallback suggested under the tanp block ('use tanp_eigen_lb in the live tanp_sq_sconstraint') does not work as stated. lbA there bounds tanp u ** tanp u, which is <= tanp u when norm u <= 1. So eigen_lb of tanp u does not transfer by monotonicity; only the hyperplane witness is shared.\n(c) The repository is being edited concurrently: the market layer is deleted and Value_Function_Uniqueness is modified. Line numbers in Uniqueness have shifted (iexit_val_visc_subsol is now at 614, not 644). The five theories of this cluster are unchanged, so every listed line is still correct and covered.\n(d) Aside for G-B: 'the mean of an integrable random vector a.s. in a closed convex set lies in the set' is absent from HOL-Analysis, HOL-Probability and the AFP. It is a candidate library root when it is extracted from the live exit_class_Y_stopped_mean_sconstraint.

## wm_cps_small

**Classifier summary.** Of independent interest in this cluster:
- **(a) Independent increments of Brownian motion (`bm_increments_indep`).** The Wiener_Measure ROOT promises it and nothing else states it. Restate it as AFP `indep_increments bm_coord` and carry it to the continuous Brownian motion with AFP `modification_indep_increments`. Its three list helpers stay with it.
- **(b) `|B_t|² − n t` is a martingale (`martingale_cbmX_square`), also a ROOT promise.** Keep it as an ~8-line corollary of the live coordinate version. The 275-line bmX norm route that proves it today goes.
- **(c) The Gaussian convolution law with degenerate variances (`gauss_measure_conv`).** Restate it with HOL's `⋆`.
- **(d) Basic martingale facts, misplaced in CPS.** These are constant expectation (generalise to banach and derive the live `martingale_mean_zero_of_start`), orthogonality of increments, and the compensator identity `E[(ΔX)²] = E[ΔA]`. All three move to CTM.
- **(e) Library API.** The `path_law` interface, and the general closed-set Portmanteau `weak_conv_closed_full_measure`, of which the live path-space `weak_conv_closed_full_mass` should become an instance.

Not of independent interest:
- the bounded-package corollaries of the `_mom` tail bounds with the hard-wired `8C²`;
- the abandoned uniform-partition tail route;
- the market-layer test functionals and `confined_paths`;
- library twins: `holder_onI_bound` (AFP `holder_onI`), `sq_tail_*` (CTM `tail_le_moment`), `bm_indicator_integral` (HOL `integral_indicator`), `open_hit_strictly_before` (live `pexit_sublevel_open`);
- the `dist` restatement of Arzelà–Ascoli;
- the set-integral compensator bridges.

Two items depend on other clusters:
- `pexit_cap_eq` stays only if class_dp keeps `exit_val_dpp_le_of_cond` for the D4 pilot.
- `etime_shift_superlevel_closed` stays only if the toolkit keeps `vshift_path_law`.

About 541 of the KEEP_API lines are not dead code at all. They are interpretation-, `fun`- or locale-generated facts, or the collective names of two-part lemmas whose parts are live, so nothing can be deleted there.

Line totals (extent to the next command):
| verdict | lines | detail |
|---|---:|---|
| KEEP_API | ≈656 | ≈541 no-op (generated facts, collective names); ≈115 genuine keeps (`path_law` API 58, `weak_conv_closed_full_measure` 53, `sets_ipath_law` 4) |
| FIX_THEN_KEEP | ≈370 | `bm_increments_indep` 186, `Sorted_Lists` helpers 74, `gauss_measure_conv` 46, `martingale_expectation_eq` 19, `increment_second_moment_bound` 45 |
| COROLLARY | ≈341 | 78 kept as short corollaries (`martingale_cbmX_square`, `expectation_increment_sq`) shrinking to ≈15 lines; ≈263 deleted, of which `pexit_cap_eq` (21) and `etime_shift_superlevel_closed` (64) depend on the other clusters' decisions |
| DELETE | ≈593 | |

**Verifier.** The classification is sound. I re-read all 46 items against the sources, HOL/HOL-Probability, the AFP (Kolmogorov_Chentsov, Martingales, Levy_Prokhorov_Metric) and the paper session. 45 verdicts are confirmed and every listed line is covered exactly once. One verdict changes: weak_conv_closed_full_measure goes from KEEP_API to COROLLARY. The general closed-set Portmanteau it was credited with is already AFP weak_conv_on_eq1, so it shrinks to a short full-mass corollary of that lemma, and the live path-space weak_conv_closed_full_mass becomes an instance of it.

Checks behind the confirmed independent-interest keeps:
- bm_increments_indep (FIX_THEN_KEEP). There is no Brownian motion in HOL or the AFP. AFP Stochastic_Processes.indep_increments uses non-strict `sorted` lists, so the restatement must handle the zero increments from duplicate times. modification_indep_increments then carries it to the continuous process. The Sorted_Lists helpers have no HOL or AFP twin (grep for Max/last facts on sorted lists is empty).
- gauss_measure_conv (FIX_THEN_KEEP). Its left-hand side is literally HOL-Probability's `convolution` (⋆, Convolution.thy:14), and HOL has only add_indep_normal, for positive variances in `distributed` form. The live gauss_measure_conv_nn plus nn_integral_convolution or convolution_emeasure' give the short proof.
- martingale_expectation_eq (FIX_THEN_KEEP). The AFP Martingales entry has only set_integral_eq, not the constant-expectation statement.

Checks behind the confirmed DELETE and COROLLARY verdicts:
- holder_onI_bound is AFP holder_onI up to dist_commute.
- bm_indicator_integral is HOL's simp rule integral_indicator.
- open_hit_strictly_before twins pexit_sublevel_open.
- pexit_stable_above_T follows from pexit_min_horizon (Path_Exit_Times:1649).
- The bounded-package tail corollaries and fourth_moment_bound_subinterval have no use outside each other.

Minor inaccuracies that do not change any verdict:
- bmX_sq has no user in Relative_Arbitrage, since Brownian_Market is already staged for deletion.
- dyadic_level_tail_mom also has the 8C² constant built into its moment hypothesis (G-E).
- path_law_restrict (KEEP_API, medium) is defensible but weak. Its only stated purpose was the projective limit deleted under D6. A more natural API lemma would be the ipath_law-to-path_law restriction that Path_Space_Infinite's text promises but never proves.
